Under bounded local oscillation, we derive a local dimension-dependent bound on |f-g|, linking Gaussian smoothing to the stability analysis of discontinuous networks. We compute closed-form Gaussian averages of the rectified linear unit (ReLU) and sign activation functions, and illustrate the mechanism on a high-dimensional binary perceptron, where layer-preactivation aggregation under an explicit quantization-noise surrogate yields the Gaussian envelope used in inference-side smoothing and training-side smooth surrogate gradients.

</details>


### [73] [Self-organizing Architecture of Receptron Units: a Hardware-Aware Framework for Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20162)
*Stefano Radice,Ludovico Casaccia,Riccaro Emanuele Beccalli,Bruno Paroli,Paolo Milani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Receptron模型的类脑分类器，该单单元架构无需多层网络即可实现非线性决策边界，适合部署在中端微控制器上并支持持续在线自适应，在基准数据集上达到与标准机器学习方法相当的精度，为资源受限的神经形态边缘系统提供了可行且可解释的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网边缘设备受限于微控制器单元极低的计算和存储能力，传统深度学习方法难以部署，亟需一种轻量级、可在线自适应且具备非线性分类能力的方案。

Method: 采用基于Receptron模型的类脑启发式分类器，这是一种单单元架构，无需多层网络即可实现非线性可分的决策边界，专为中端MCU设计且支持持续在线自适应。

Result: 在基本基准数据集上进行交叉验证，准确率与标准机器学习方法基线相当。

Conclusion: Receptron可作为资源受限、动态非平稳环境下神经形态边缘系统的可行且可解释的替代方案。

Abstract: The growing demand for intelligent processing at the edge of IoT networks is constrained by the severe computational and memory limitations of microcontroller units, which render impractical conventional deep learning approaches. We propose a neuromorphicinspired classifier based on the Receptron model, a single-unit architecture capable of implementing non-linearly separable decision boundaries, without resorting to multi-layer networks. The model is designed for direct deployment on mid-range MCUs, while supporting continuous on-device adaptation. Experimental evaluation on basic dataset benchmarks yields cross-validated accuracies compatible with standard machine learning method baselines. These results position the Receptron as a viable and interpretable alternative for resource-constrained neuromorphic edge systems operating in dynamic, non-stationary environments.

</details>


### [74] [Instance Hardness-Based Relevance for Imbalanced Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20173)
*Vitor M. Leitao,Juscimara G. Avelino,George D. C. Cavalcanti,Rafael M. O. Cruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于实例难度的相关函数（InHaR），用于识别回归问题中的稀有实例。与传统依赖目标变量分布的相关函数不同，InHaR通过引入学习难度来推断稀有性，尤其适用于双峰分布等复杂场景。实验表明，InHaR能正确识别稀有区域，并显著提升随机过采样和高斯噪声等重采样策略的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统回归中的相关函数仅基于目标变量值分配固定重要性，在双峰分布等复杂场景下无法区分真正稀有实例与正常实例，导致不平衡学习方法效果受限。

Method: 提出Instance Hardness-based relevance function（InHaR），将实例的学习难度纳入稀有性推断，使稀有性不仅依赖于目标分布，还依赖于学习算法对实例的拟合困难程度。

Result: InHaR在双峰分布下能正确识别稀有区域；将其用于指导随机过采样和高斯噪声重采样策略时，相比传统相关函数方法，预测性能有显著提升。

Conclusion: 基于实例难度的相关函数能更准确地识别回归中的稀有实例，为不平衡回归问题提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Imbalanced regression problems arise when the target variable has an asymmetric distribution, resulting in underrepresented value ranges in the dataset. Traditional approaches for identifying rare instances rely on a relevance function that assigns higher importance to specific regions of the target distribution. However, the effectiveness of imbalance-aware learning methods depends strongly on how relevance is defined. In more complex scenarios, such as bimodal distributions, traditional relevance functions struggle to capture rarity, as they assign fixed relevance values based solely on target values, thereby compromising the distinction between truly rare and normal instances. To address these limitations, this study proposes an Instance Hardness-based relevance function (InHaR) for identifying rare instances in regression problems. Unlike traditional relevance functions, the proposed approach incorporates learning difficulty, allowing rarity to be inferred not only from the target distribution but also from the difficulty of instances for the learning algorithm. This property is particularly important in bimodal scenarios, where rarity cannot be accurately inferred from target values alone. Experimental results demonstrate that the InHaR correctly identifies rare regions under bimodal distributions and, when used to guide resampling strategies such as Random Oversampling (RO) and Gaussian Noise (GN), leads to significant improvements in predictive performance compared to traditional relevance-based approaches. The code, dataset, and further details about the proposed method are publicly available at https://github.com/VitorLeitao/instance-hardness-Imbalanced-regression.

</details>


### [75] [On Optimization Complexity of Second-Order Certified Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20192)
*Nikita Doikov,Anastasia Koloskova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从优化角度研究机器遗忘（machine unlearning）的算法复杂度，证明了若被删除数据能被遗忘模型良好预测，则对应优化问题简单；并提出一种基于各向异性高斯机制的二阶遗忘算法，在线性模型和拟自和谐损失下实现了最先进的全局收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘方法缺乏对遗忘算法复杂度的理论理解，尤其从优化角度出发的认证遗忘（certified unlearning）与优化精度之间的权衡关系尚未被系统研究。

Method: 1) 利用一致凸正则化（uniformly convex regularizers）的概念，提出一种替代泛化误差的新上界来刻画初始模型与遗忘模型之间的距离；2) 开发了一种基于各向异性高斯机制的二阶遗忘算法，用于线性模型和拟自和谐损失（quasi-self-concordant losses）的认证遗忘。

Result: 理论上证明：若被移除数据能被遗忘模型良好预测，则对应的优化问题简单；所提出的二阶算法在认证遗忘中达到快速收敛率（fast rates），并且相比一阶遗忘方法具有可证明的优势。

Conclusion: 本文为机器遗忘提供了优化视角下的理论保障，证明了利用二阶信息在遗忘任务中的优越性，并涵盖逻辑回归、指数回归等具体应用场景。

Abstract: We study machine unlearning: the removal of memorized training data from a trained model. Specifically, we investigate the algorithmic complexity of certified unlearning from an optimization perspective. We formalize the goal of an unlearning algorithm as simultaneously achieving certified unlearning and optimization accuracy. Utilizing the notion of uniformly convex regularizers, we prove new bounds on the distance between initial and unlearned models using a novel substitute for generalization error. Thus we theoretically demonstrate that if the removed data is well-predicted by the unlearned model, the corresponding optimization problem is simple. Furthermore, we develop a new second-order unlearning algorithm with an anisotropic Gaussian mechanism and state-of-the-art global convergence. We prove fast rates for our method in achieving certified unlearning for linear models with quasi-self-concordant losses. As a direct application, our theory covers unlearning for logistic and exponential regressions and shows a provable benefit of utilizing second-order information compared to first-order unlearning methods.

</details>


### [76] [OLEDLM: A Unified Language Model for OLED Molecular Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20194)
*Fukang Wen,Yuchong Tang,Jingyuan Li,Beichen Wang,Yixuan Jiang,Xiaoyi Jiang,Yaxuan Liu,Shunyu Wang,Zuoqiang Shi,Yi Zhu,Yanan Zhu,Pipi Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于因果语言模型的逆分子设计框架，用于生成满足特定光电性质约束的OLED SMILES序列。采用多阶段策略：基于LLaMA架构建立基础化学语言模型，这是首次将LLM成功适配于OLED领域；微调基于BERT的性质预测器；进行强化学习优化SMILES生成；DFT验证表明框架能高效生成结构有效且光电性质优化的新型OLED候选分子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OLED材料开发面临化学空间巨大、量子化学约束严苛以及标记数据稀缺等多重挑战，而针对该领域的有效分子生成模型很少。

Method: 采用多阶段策略：(1) 基于LLaMA架构构建OLED专用基础化学语言模型；(2) 在大规模OLED数据集上预训练BERT模型并微调性质预测器；(3) 利用性质预测器对微调模型进行强化学习以优化SMILES生成；(4) 通过DFT验证评估生成结果。

Result: 该框架能够高效导航OLED化学空间，生成结构有效且光电性质（如激发能、振子强度）优化的新型候选分子。

Conclusion: 首次成功将LLM适配于OLED领域，弥合了通用分子生成与光电材料严格结构要求之间的差距，证明了基于因果语言模型的逆分子设计在OLED领域的有效性。

Abstract: The development of organic light-emitting diode (OLED) materials faces the compounded challenges of an astronomically large chemical space, stringent quantum-chemical constraints, and a scarcity of labeled data. Although the question of OLED generation is important, few models have been trained effectively for this specific domain. We propose an inverse molecular design framework based on causal language models: given target optoelectronic properties (e.g., excitation energy, oscillator strength), our model directly generates OLED SMILES sequences satisfying the specified constraints. We employ a multi-stage strategy: first, we establish a foundational chemical language model using a LLaMA-style transformer architecture. To the best of our knowledge, this represents the first successful adaptation of LLMs specifically for the OLED domain, bridging the gap between generic molecular generation and the stringent structural requirements of optoelectronic materials. Second, we fine-tune property predictors based on a BERT model pre-trained on our large-scale OLED dataset. Then, we perform Reinforcement Learning on our fine-tuned model, leveraging our property predictor, for better SMILES generation. Finally, through DFT verification, we demonstrate that our framework can efficiently navigate the OLED chemical space, generating novel candidates with high structural validity and optimized optoelectronic properties.

</details>


### [77] [The Quadrilateral Loss: Additivity as a Measurable Behavior of Dense Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20201)
*Antonio Di Cecco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出四边形损失（quadrilateral loss）作为可微分的可加性惩罚项，将可加性从架构约束转变为可测量的行为，实现交互程度的可控调节与在线观测，并系统比较了多种实现严格可加性的路径，揭示了三种静默失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可加模型通过禁止特征交互来获得可解释性，但传统方法通过架构强制实现这一约束。本文希望将可加性从硬性架构约束转变为可量化的、可调节的行为约束，以便更灵活地控制交互程度并观察其影响。

Method: 提出四边形损失，基于训练点对中交换一个坐标的二阶混合差分，当且仅当该坐标无交互时为零；该损失与干预Shapley-GAM的每坐标交互质量在期望上等价。在此基础上进行系统性实验比较：结构掩码、行为惩罚（可结晶为精确结构）、权重衰减、回拟合、共享截面模型、装袋提升树桩等多种实现严格可加性的路径。

Result: 大多数学习到的交互几乎可以零成本移除；在小型数据集上适度惩罚可同时提升精度和可加性；预正则化的交互大小几乎无法预测正则化模型保留的交互，削弱了事后交互排序的可信度；不同路径之间行为约束先于结构约束优于权重空间约束，排名在不同数据体制间逆转，趋同的路径在形状函数本身达成一致。

Conclusion: 可加性可作为可调参数而非硬约束使用；四边形损失为交互分析提供了新的在线观测工具；三种静默失效模式的共同特征是：将在特定条件下成立的保证迁移到了其前提条件不再满足的环境中。

Abstract: Additive models buy interpretability by forbidding feature interactions, a constraint that neural instantiations enforce architecturally. We introduce the quadrilateral loss, a differentiable penalty that treats additivity as a measurable behavior instead: a second-order mixed difference on pairs of training points swapping one coordinate, which vanishes if and only if the coordinate carries no interaction, remains informative for piecewise-linear networks, and equals in expectation the per-coordinate interaction mass of the interventional Shapley-GAM. The loss turns additivity into a dial - most learned interactions prove removable almost for free, and on small datasets a moderate penalty improves accuracy and additivity simultaneously - and into an online observable: its per-feature surrender curves show, across seeds and datasets, that pre-regularization interaction magnitude barely predicts what a regularized model retains, undermining post-hoc interaction rankings. Against this instrument we compare routes to exact additivity, spanning structural masks, behavioral penalties (optionally crystallized into exact structure), weight decay, backfitting, the shared-section model, and bagged boosted stumps: constraining behavior before structure dominates weight-space constraints, rankings reverse between data regimes, and converging routes agree on the shape functions themselves. Three silent failure modes we document share one anatomy: guarantees imported into settings that quietly void their preconditions.

</details>


### [78] [ELSAA: Efficient Low-Rank and Sparse Attention Approximation for Training Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20214)
*Mahdi Heidari,Mohammad Mahdi Rahimi,Jaekyun Moon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ELSAA提出了一种低秩+稀疏的注意力近似方法，在不实例化完整二次评分矩阵的情况下，通过稀疏分支捕获高相似性交互、低秩分支汇总全局扩散交互，并引入分母感知融合项来归一化两个分支，以支持长上下文训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer中二次复杂度的注意力评分矩阵是扩展输入长度的核心障碍，现有方法要么施加稀疏性（每查询仅关注少量键），要么使用低秩/核方法压缩全局交互。ELSAA旨在同时结合两者的优势，且不分解投影或输出矩阵，而是直接近似注意力评分算子本身。

Method: ELSAA在密集投影生成Q、K、V后，直接近似注意力评分算子：稀疏分支捕获选中的高相似性交互，低秩分支汇总扩散的全局交互。由于两个分支的归一化支撑集差异很大，引入分母感知融合项，根据稀疏分支相对于低秩分支的估计注意力质量来缩放稀疏分支。

Result: ELSAA提供了一种无需实例化完整二次评分矩阵即可构建低秩和稀疏注意力输出的实用框架，旨在支持更长上下文的训练，同时保留尖锐的token级交互和广泛的上下文混合。

Conclusion: ELSAA通过直接近似注意力算子而非分解投影矩阵，结合稀疏和低秩两条分支并引入分母感知融合，为高效长上下文Transformer提供了实用方案。

Abstract: The quadratic $N\times N$ attention score matrix remains a central obstacle to extending Transformers to longer input lengths. Existing efficient attention methods usually reduce this bottleneck by either imposing sparsity, so that each query attends to only a small subset of keys, or by using low-rank/kernel sketches, so that global interactions are compressed into a lower-dimensional representation. We propose \emph{ELSAA}, an efficient low-rank and sparse approximation of attention. Importantly, ELSAA does \emph{not} decompose the learned projection or output matrices of the Transformer into sparse and low-rank factors. Instead, after dense projections produce $Q,K,V$, ELSAA approximates the induced attention score operator itself: a sparse branch captures selected high-similarity interactions, while a low-rank branch summarizes diffuse global interactions. Since the two branches can be normalized over supports with very different denominator mass, ELSAA introduces a denominator-aware fusion term that scales the sparse branch according to its estimated attention mass relative to the low-rank branch. This gives a practical framework for constructing low-rank and sparse attention outputs without materializing the full quadratic score matrix, aiming to enable longer-context training while preserving both sharp token-level interactions and broad contextual mixing.

</details>


### [79] [User-Centric Modeling of Transactional Sequences with Explainable State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20228)
*Ivan Palagin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合对比表示学习(CoLES)与状态空间模型(Mamba)的混合方法用于用户建模，通过两种集成策略提升性能，在三个数据集上取得一致改进，收敛速度提升2-3倍，并进行了可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的用户序列编码器（RNN存在梯度消失问题，Transformer存在二次复杂度问题）无法高效处理长距离依赖，而Mamba虽能解决这些问题但尚未被探索用于个性化用户分析。

Method: 采用两种集成策略：(1) 用CoLES嵌入初始化Mamba隐藏状态；(2) 将投影后的CoLES嵌入作为前缀标记添加到输入序列前。两种方法都从第一步起为模型提供信息丰富的用户先验。

Result: 在Age（多分类年龄组预测）、MBD（多标签产品购买）、Taobao（二值购买预测）三个公开数据集上，混合模型一致优于单独的Mamba和带线性分类器的CoLES，且收敛速度比纯SSM基线快2-3倍。

Conclusion: 混合对比表示学习与状态空间模型的方法能有效提升用户序列建模性能，并通过离散化步长图和集成梯度实现可解释性分析，揭示行为丰富数据集上的选择性事件过滤机制和最信息量的交易特征。

Abstract: We propose a hybrid approach for user-centric modeling of transactional event sequences that combines contrastive representation learning (CoLES) with State Space Models (SSMs). While contrastive methods yield high-quality compressed user representations, existing encoders -- RNNs and Transformers -- suffer from vanishing gradients or quadratic complexity, respectively. Mamba, a selective SSM, efficiently handles long-range dependencies but remains underexplored for personalized user analysis. We investigate two integration strategies: (1)~initializing the Mamba hidden state with a CoLES embedding, and (2)~prepending the projected CoLES embedding as a prefix token to the input sequence. Both approaches supply the model with an informative user prior from the first step. Experiments on three public datasets -- Age (multiclass age-group prediction), MBD (multi-label product acquisition), and Taobao (binary purchase prediction) -- demonstrate consistent improvements over standalone Mamba and CoLES with a linear classifier, with the hybrid models converging 2--3$\times$ faster than the plain SSM baseline. Explainability analysis via discretization-step maps and Integrated Gradients reveals selective event filtering on behavior-rich datasets and identifies the most informative transaction features.

</details>


### [80] [PIER: Physics-Informed Environmental Retrieval for Time-Series Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20230)
*Shiyuan Luo,Runlong Yu,Chonghao Qiu,Yue Qin,Rahul Ghosh,Robert Ladwig,Paul C. Hanson,Yiqun Xie,Xiaowei Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PIER是一个模型无关的检索增强框架，通过引入物理感知流（基于通量-响应一致性的局部验证器）和自适应权重调整机制，在环境系统建模中实现了比标准嵌入检索更准确的水温和溶解氧预测，并在356个湖泊41年数据上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境系统建模中观测数据有限，物理动态特性随系统变化，标准嵌入检索无法保证检索结果与目标场景的物理过程一致性，因为相似嵌入可能对应不同的物理机制。

Method: PIER框架包含三个核心组件：①物理感知检索流——使用基于物理导出的通量特征训练的局部验证器，根据通量-响应一致性对候选场景评分；②权重调整机制——学习每个场景的诊断特征，自适应平衡嵌入检索流与物理感知流的权重；③模型无关的集成策略，可适用于不同主干模型。

Result: 在美国中西部356个湖泊41年的数据上，PIER在水温和溶解氧预测任务中一致优于基线方法，并且可作为通用增强策略适用于多种不同主干模型。

Conclusion: 通过引入物理一致性约束，PIER有效弥补了纯嵌入检索的局限，为跨系统知识迁移提供了一种兼顾计算效率与物理可靠性的通用框架。

Abstract: Accurate modeling of environmental systems is fundamental to scientific understanding and decision-making, yet remains challenging because observations are limited and physical dynamics vary across systems. Retrieval-augmented approaches offer a natural path to transfer knowledge across systems, but standard embedding-based retrieval does not guarantee consistency of underlying physical processes, since scenarios with similar embeddings may arise from different underlying mechanisms. We propose Physics-Informed Environmental Retrieval (PIER), a model-agnostic framework that augments embedding-based retrieval with a physics-aware stream that scores candidates by flux-response consistency with the target, using local verifiers trained on physics-derived flux features. A weight adjustment mechanism then learns per-scenario weights that adaptively balance the two retrieval streams based on diagnostic features summarizing physics-stream reliability. Experiments on 356 lakes across the Midwestern United States spanning 41 years show that PIER consistently outperforms baselines for water temperature and dissolved oxygen prediction, and serves as a general augmentation strategy across diverse backbones.

</details>


### [81] [PhaseAware: Interpretable Human-in-the-Loop Rehabilitation Scoring with Boundary Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20237)
*Yankai Zheng,Yuhe Liu,Yuxin Ma,Tianci Xue,Jiayuan Tian,Yu Fu,Yuxuan Hu,Jianing Wang,Zichun Xiao,Junya Mu,Shaohui Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PhaseAware是一个轻量级康复质量评估框架，通过骨干网络结合相位与身体分组描述符，在UI-PRMD和KIMORE数据集上实现高精度评分（RMSE 0.0230，相较基线降低88.9%），同时生成结构化审查线索以支持临床工作流中的可解释性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有康复评分系统缺乏在临床工作流中可审查和解释的能力，亟需一种既能提供连续质量评估、又能生成可解释性线索以支持临床医生审核的紧凑型框架。

Method: 提出PhaseAware框架，采用时间骨干网络结合相位和身体分组描述符，通过骨干条件门控残差路径稳定特征表示；在UI-PRMD深蹲协议上评估并在KIMORE蹲姿子集上验证迁移性；基于相位和身体级别敏感性生成结构化审查线索。

Result: 在UI-PRMD上RMSE为0.0230，相较基线降低88.9%；在KIMORE上保持良好性能，表明相位感知设计可跨协议迁移；生成的审查线索可突出显示对预测最相关的运动阶段和身体区域。

Conclusion: PhaseAware提供了一种实用且可解释的康复评分方法，有助于将自动评估集成到信息系统中的同时保留临床医生的监督权，支持临床审核、边界案例监控和人在回路分流。

Abstract: Rehabilitation scoring systems are most useful when their outputs can be reviewed and interpreted within clinical workflows. This study presents PhaseAware, a compact framework for continuous rehabilitation quality assessment that combines a temporal backbone with phase- and body-group descriptors through a backbone-conditioned gated residual pathway. The model was evaluated on the UI-PRMD deep-squat protocol and further tested on the KIMORE squatting subset. On UI-PRMD, PhaseAware achieved an RMSE of 0.0230, corresponding to an 88.9% reduction relative to the accepted baseline. It also maintained favorable performance on KIMORE, suggesting that the phase-aware design transfers across related squatting protocols. In addition to score prediction, PhaseAware generates structured review cues based on phase- and body-level sensitivity, highlighting the movement stages and body regions most relevant to each prediction. The architecture employs a backbone-conditioned gated residual mechanism to stabilize feature representation, supporting use in resource-constrained settings. These cues are intended to support clinician review, boundary-case monitoring, and human-in-the-loop triage rather than autonomous decision-making. Overall, PhaseAware offers a practical and interpretable approach to rehabilitation scoring that may help integrate automated assessment into information systems while preserving clinician oversight.

</details>


### [82] [Breaking the $T^{3/4}$ Barrier for Regret Minimization With Bi-Dimensional CDFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20258)
*Matteo Castiglioni,Anna Lunghi,Alberto Marchesi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在[0,1]²上学习CDF相关目标函数g(x)·P(X≤x)的遗憾最小化问题，提出算法达到Ẽ(T^{7/10})遗憾界，优于此前最优的Ẽ(T^{3/4})，但仍有下界Ω(T^{2/3})的差距。该技术应用于双边贸易利润最大化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对CDF相关目标函数g(x)·P(X≤x)的在线学习问题，现有算法遗憾界为Ẽ(T^{3/4})，本文希望进一步缩小与下界Ω(T^{2/3})之间的差距，探索维度诅咒是否可以部分被克服。

Method: 设计了一种新的在线学习算法，通过精细的探索与利用策略，在二维空间[0,1]²上实现了更优的遗憾上界Ẽ(T^{7/10})。

Result: 遗憾界从Ẽ(T^{3/4})提升至Ẽ(T^{7/10})，但仍与下界Ω(T^{2/3})存在差距。

Conclusion: 该结果表明CDF相关目标函数的维度诅咒可以被部分缓解，但下界尚未达到；该技术还可推广至重复双边贸易中的固定价格利润最大化问题，获得相同的遗憾界。

Abstract: We study regret minimization for learning CDF-related objectives of the form \[ g(x)\cdot\mathbb{P}_{X\sim\mathcal{D}}(X\le x), \] over $[0,1]^2$, where $g$ is a known Lipschitz function and $\mathcal{D}$ is an unknown distribution. At each round $t$, the learner selects a point $x_t$ and observes the binary feedback $\mathbb{I}(X_t\le x_t)$, where $X_t\sim\mathcal{D}$. We design an algorithm achieving regret $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{7/10})$, improving over the previous best-known bound of $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{3/4})$ and showing that the curse of dimensionality can be at least partially lifted for this class of objectives, though a gap remains with the $Ω(T^{2/3})$ lower bound. As an application, our techniques yield the same $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{7/10})$ regret bound for profit maximization in repeated bilateral trade with fixed prices.

</details>


### [83] [Interpretable Fuzzy Rule-Based Regression Extension for Ex-Fuzzy Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20277)
*Cayan Deniz Kucuktopana,Javier Fumanal-Idocin,Richard Pitts,Javier Andreu-Perez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为Ex-Fuzzy库提出了可解释的回归扩展，采用基于模糊C均值聚类的目标感知分区初始化策略，在10个KEEL数据集上评估表明高斯分区优于均匀梯形分区，实现了约0.86的判定系数和10-15条可读规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在回归任务中预测精度高，但在安全关键和受监管领域部署需要可解释性。虽然模糊规则系统提供透明、语言可解释的模型，但Mamdani型模糊回归在现代机器学习软件库中仍然代表性不足。

Method: 为Ex-Fuzzy库开发可解释回归扩展，实现Mamdani模糊推理与标量后件从数据直接学习；提出基于模糊C均值聚类的目标感知分区初始化策略，从增广输入-输出空间中推导语言变量以强调特征空间中与输出相关的区域。

Result: 在10个KEEL回归数据集上，高斯分区策略一致优于均匀梯形分区策略，平均判定系数约0.86，生成10-15条人类可读的紧凑规则库。

Conclusion: 所提出的实现为黑箱回归模型提供了透明且有竞争力的替代方案，在保持竞争性预测性能的同时支持实际可解释性。

Abstract: Machine learning models achieve high predictive accuracy in regression tasks, but their deployment in safety-critical and regulated domains requires interpretability. While fuzzy rule-based systems offer transparent, linguistically explicit interpretable models, Mamdani-style fuzzy regression remains underrepresented in modern machine learning software libraries. This paper presents an interpretable regression extension for the Ex-Fuzzy library, enabling Mamdani fuzzy inference with scalar consequents learned directly from data. For this, a target-aware partition initialisation strategy based on Fuzzy C-Means clustering is introduced, in which linguistic variables are derived from an augmented input-output space to emphasise output-relevant regions of the feature space. The proposed extension is evaluated on ten regression datasets from the KEEL repository, comparing Gaussian and trapezoidal partition strategies against standard baselines including linear regression, multilayer perceptron, and random forests. Experimental results show that Gaussian partitions consistently outperform uniform trapezoidal partitions, achieving a mean coefficient of determination of approximately 0.86 while producing compact rule bases of 10-15 human-readable rules. The proposed implementation provides a transparent and competitive alternative to black-box regression models, supporting practical interpretability with competitive predictive performance.

</details>


### [84] [The Blessing of Dimensionality: How Near-Orthogonality in High-Dimensional Spaces Explains Temporal Portability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20301)
*Abigail Woodring,Adrian Chan,Rana Muhammad Shahroz Khan,Sukwon Yun,Chau-Wai Wong,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过10个连续预训练步骤的实证研究和两种理论分析，验证了PortLLM方法在长期时间跨度上的可移植性，发现高维向量的近正交性是时间可移植性的关键原因，表明在基座模型周期性更新时无需重复微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PortLLM的初始结果仅展示了LoRA补丁的短期时间可移植性，其长期性能及理论原理尚不明确。

Method: (1) 基于Mistral、Gemma、Qwen三个基座模型，在10个连续预训练步骤上对PortLLM补丁的长期时间可移植性进行实证研究；(2) 提供两种理论分析解释PortLLM的竞争力。

Result: 可移植性在更长时间跨度上持续存在，重复微调在基座模型周期性更新时并非必需；高维向量的近正交性是时间可移植性的关键理论依据；损失景观的几何视角有助于不同适应方案的理论比较。

Conclusion: PortLLM在长期时间跨度上依然有效，其理论基础在于高维向量的近正交性，为无需重复微调的模型适配提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Fine-tuning has been widely used to adapt large language models (LLMs) for domain-specific tasks. Parameter efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods such as low-rank adaptation (LoRA) are frequently used to reduce computational costs. PortLLM is a training-free and data-free scheme used to adapt LLMs after continual pretraining. Although the initial PortLLM results show that LoRA patches exhibit short-term temporal portability, the long-term performance of PortLLM across several updates of continual pretraining remains underexplored. Furthermore, the intriguing effectiveness of PortLLM is not well understood from a theoretical standpoint. We address these two open questions by (1) performing an extensive empirical study of the long-term temporal portability of PortLLM patches across 10 continual pretraining steps using base models Mistral, Gemma, and Qwen; and (2) offering two theoretical analyses to explain our observation that the simple PortLLM method achieves competitive performance. We find empirically that the portability persists across longer time duration, indicating that repeated fine-tuning is not required when the base model is periodically updated. We find theoretically that near-orthogonality of high-dimensional vectors is a key justification for temporal portability. Our analyses also demonstrate a geometric perspective of the loss landscape in facilitating the theoretical comparison of different adaptation options.

</details>


### [85] [Classical Hardware Acceleration of Quantum Autoencoders for Real-Time Anomaly Detection in Collider Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20302)
*Ivan Ge,Sagar Addepalli,Abhilasha Dave,Julia Gonski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了变分量子自编码器模型在实时对撞机异常检测触发中的应用，模型性能媲美经典方法，且经FPGA综合后满足资源与时序约束，首次实现了用于高能物理触发的QML模型的FPGA部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理中的量子机器学习算法能够高效表示高维对撞数据中的长程高阶关联，且参数更少、扩展性更优。但将其部署到实时触发系统需要能够经典地模拟和编译量子电路，并在低延迟FPGA硬件上综合实现量子门操作。现有工作缺乏这类FPGA实现。

Method: 采用变分量子自编码器（VQAE）模型进行实时异常检测触发；通过经典模拟和编译量子电路，将量子门综合到FPGA硬件加速器上，满足资源使用和时序约束。

Result: 模型性能与最先进的经典方法相当；经FPGA综合后，资源使用和时序约束均满足未来对撞机触发应用的要求。提供了首个面向HEP触发的QML模型的FPGA实现。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了QML模型在经典数据采集管线中实现更高能力模型的可行性，同时推动了对撞机实验基础设施的量子就绪性。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning (QML) algorithms in high energy physics (HEP) can efficiently represent and leverage long-range, high-order correlations in high-dimensional collider data, potentially with fewer parameters and favorable scaling relative to classical models. Deployment of QML in real-time collider applications such as trigger systems requires the ability to emulate and compile quantum circuits classically, then synthesize the resulting quantum gates onto low-latency hardware accelerators, namely field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs). We present a study of variational quantum autoencoder models for real-time anomaly detection triggers in modern collider experiments. The models achieve performance comparable to state-of-the-art classical approaches and, after FPGA synthesis, satisfy resource usage and timing constraints consistent with trigger applications in future colliders. This work provides one of the first FPGA implementations of QML models for HEP triggers, enabling higher-capability models in today's classical data acquisition pipelines while advancing quantum readiness of collider experiment infrastructure.

</details>


### [86] [Multi-modal transformer for signal classification in nanopore blockade experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20323)
*Sandro Kuppel,Julian Hoßbach,Samuel Tovey,Christian Holm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多模态深度学习架构，联合处理原始时间序列、小波图像和静态特征向量三种信号表示，在42肽基准测试中准确率提升超10个百分点，并在20氨基酸数据集上达到近乎完美精度，显著提升了纳米孔传感器的分子识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米孔传感器产生的离子电流信号高度复杂，将信号可靠地分配给特定分子是当前的主要挑战，亟需更强大的信号解析方法。

Method: 提出多模态深度学习架构，联合处理三种信号表示：原始时间序列数据、基于小波变换的图像以及静态特征向量，利用注意力机制融合互补信息。

Result: 在42肽基准测试中超越现有方法超10个百分点，在20氨基酸数据集上达到近乎完美准确率。

Conclusion: 多模态深度学习能够整合纳米孔传感器的互补信号信息，实现鲁棒、高精度的分子识别，展现了机器学习在纳米孔传感中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Nanopore devices have emerged as powerful tools for single-molecule sensing, with potential for rapid, portable diagnostics. They detect changes in ionic current as analytes enter nanometer-scale pores, providing a means of identifying diverse biomarkers from their characteristic signal patterns. However, these signals are highly complex, and reliably assigning them to specific molecules remains a major challenge. Here, we address this by introducing a multi-modal deep learning architecture that jointly processes multiple signal representations, including raw time-series data, wavelet-based images, and static feature vectors. Our approach surpasses existing methods by more than 10 percentage points on a 42-peptide benchmark and transfers to a 20-amino-acid dataset with near-perfect accuracy. The model integrates complementary information from these representations, with attention analysis showing that the time-series and wavelet-image inputs emphasize different features of the same event. Together, these results demonstrate the potential of machine learning to enable robust, high-accuracy molecular identification with nanopore sensors.

</details>


### [87] [Interval and fuzzy physics-augmented neural networks (iPANN and fPANN) for uncertainty quantification and propagation in constitutive modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20339)
*Somesh Pratap Singh,Govinda Anantha Padmanabha,Jingye Tan,Steven Yang,Reese E. Jones,D. Thomas Seidl,Nikolaos Bouklas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出区间物理增强神经网络(iPANNs)和模糊物理增强神经网络(fPANNs)两类模型，用于超弹性本构建模中的无分布偶然不确定性量化。iPANNs学习稀疏的下界、均值和上界自由能密度分支，通过自动微分获得应力以包围含噪声的应力观测；fPANNs通过α-截集插值将iPANNs分支嵌入模糊集表示。模型编码了客观性、一致性和多凸性等力学约束，并采用平滑L0正则化实现可解释的能量表示。通过两阶段迁移学习训练，在合成各向同性超弹性数据上验证了方法的有效性，并展示了在有限元模拟中的不确定性传播能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据稀疏、含噪或异质的情况下，不确定性下的本构建模仍是可靠力学模拟的核心挑战。现有方法缺乏一种既能保证物理一致性又能实现无分布不确定性量化的紧凑模型。

Method: 提出iPANNs和fPANNs。iPANNs学习下界、均值和上界三条自由能密度分支，通过自动微分得到应力边界。fPANNs通过α-截集插值构建模糊集。模型编码客观性、一致性，促进多凸性，使用平滑L0正则化。采用两阶段迁移学习训练。

Result: 在具有异方差噪声、不同随机实现、偏移噪声均值和不同噪声幅度的合成各向同性超弹性数据上，学习到的边界包围了含噪应力观测并在测试集上泛化。在有限元设置中实现了不确定性传播。

Conclusion: 该框架为超弹性本构建模中的无分布偶然不确定性量化及下游有限元模拟中的不确定性传播提供了一条紧凑、物理一致的途径。

Abstract: Constitutive modeling under uncertainty remains a central challenge for reliable mechanics simulations, particularly when the available stress-deformation data are sparse, noisy, or heterogeneous. We propose interval and fuzzy physics-augmented neural networks (iPANNs and fPANNs) for uncertainty-aware hyperelastic constitutive modeling. iPANNs learn sparse lower, mean, and upper free energy density branches whose stresses, obtained by automatic differentiation, ultimately enclose noisy stress observations. In contrast to this deterministic interval description, fPANNs embed the learned iPANN branches into a fuzzy-set representation through alpha-cut interpolation, yielding a nested family of admissible responses. iPANNs and fPANNs encode mechanistic constraints - preserving objectivity, consistency and promoting polyconvexity - and smoothed L0 regularization promotes interpretable energy representations. The bound models are trained through a two-stage transfer-learning procedure in which a sparse mean constitutive response is learned first and then fine-tuned into lower and upper energy branches. We evaluate the framework on synthetic isotropic hyperelastic data with heteroscedastic noise, varying random realizations, shifted noise means, and varying noise magnitudes. The results show that the learned bounds enclose noisy stress observations while generalizing to the test set. Further, we examine the propagation of uncertainty through the mean, upper and lower bound predictions of the learned iPANN models in a finite element setting. The proposed framework provides a compact, physics-consistent route for distribution-free aleatoric uncertainty quantification in hyperelastic constitutive modeling, and propagation in downstream finite element simulations.

</details>


### [88] [Variance-reduced Domain Adaptation using Paired Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20367)
*Andrea Napoli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出配对采样域自适应方法（PSDA），通过跨域和域内配对观察形成四元组，专门针对相关性对齐和最大均值差异等分布匹配损失设计随机方差缩减技术，实验表明能有效降低梯度方差并提升目标域精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无监督域自适应中，相关性对齐和最大均值差异两种常用的分布匹配损失存在高方差问题，削弱了小批量优化中的有效性；同时这些损失缺乏有限和结构，无法兼容经典随机方差缩减方法。

Method: 提出PSDA方法：将域内和跨域观察配对形成始终一起采样的四元组，配对方式设计为最小化期望梯度方差，并转化为求解一组线性分配问题。

Result: 模拟实验显示比相关方法方差更低；在三个域偏移数据集上的实验表明目标域准确率得到提升。

Conclusion: PSDA能有效解决分布匹配损失的高方差问题，使其兼容随机方差缩减，从而提升无监督域自适应的性能。

Abstract: Correlation alignment and the maximum mean discrepancy are two widely used distribution-matching frameworks for unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA). However, high variance in these losses has been shown to undermine their effectiveness in minibatch optimisation settings. Furthermore, the losses lack finite-sum structure, which renders them incompatible with classical stochastic variance reduction (SVR) methods. This paper proposes Paired Sampling for Domain Adaptation (PSDA), a novel SVR technique tailored to such objectives. PSDA pairs observations both within and across domains, to form quadruplets that are always sampled together during training. The pairings are designed to minimise expected gradient variance, and reduce to solving a set of linear assignment problems. Our simulations demonstrate reduced variance compared to related methods, and experiments on three domain shift datasets show improved target domain accuracy.

</details>


### [89] [Online Variance Reduction for Domain Adaptation on Streaming Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20374)
*Andrea Napoli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ARROW算法，这是首个针对流式数据的在线随机方差缩减算法，用于MMD和CORAL损失函数，通过自适应重加权方式实现在线学习场景下的方差缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MMD和CORAL损失的离线SVR算法无法适用于在线、分布式或增量学习场景，需要一种能够处理流式数据的在线方差缩减方法。

Method: ARROW维护对齐统计量的移动平均参考，并对传入的小批量进行自适应重加权，使小批量统计量与参考统计量对齐；同时提出松弛重加权方案使权重优化问题可解。

Result: 实验表明ARROW在运行时间、方差缩减程度和目标域准确率方面与离线算法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: ARROW成功将MMD和CORAL的SVR扩展至在线学习场景，为流式数据上的分布对齐问题提供了有效的在线方差缩减方案。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of stochastic variance reduction (SVR) for the maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) and correlation alignment (CORAL) loss functions. Although various offline SVR algorithms for these losses have been proposed, these are incompatible with online, distributed, or incremental learning settings. This paper presents Adaptive vaRiance Reduction via Online reWeighting (ARROW), the first online SVR algorithm for the MMD and CORAL for streamed data. The method maintains moving average references of the alignment statistics, and adaptively reweights incoming minibatches so that the minibatch and reference statistics are aligned. Further, we propose a relaxed reweighting scheme so that the ensuing weight-optimisation problem is tractable. In experiments and simulations, we show that ARROW performs competitively with offline algorithms in terms of runtime, degree of variance reduction achieved, and target domain accuracy.

</details>


### [90] [PG-KINN: A Physics-Informed Petrov-Galerkin Kolmogorov-Arnold Network for Solving Forward and Inverse PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20378)
*Amirhossein Sadr,Nima Soltani,Vahideh Moghtadaiee,Aida Pakniyat,Dara Rahmati,Saeid Gorgin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PG-KINN，一种基于Petrov-Galerkin变分公式的物理信息KAN，通过将KAN试函数空间与紧支撑分段多项式测试函数空间结合，解决了传统MLP和KAN在PDE求解中的谱偏差、参数化密集、强形式残差最小化需要高阶导数等问题，在裂纹奇异性、应力集中、超弹性、逆问题等基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLP存在谱偏差和密集参数化导致的精度与可解释性限制；现有KAN的强形式残差最小化需要高阶导数和重加权损失，能量形式限于自伴算子且对参数识别问题退化为平凡解，边界积分形式需要已知基本解。因此需要一种更鲁棒且通用的物理信息框架。

Method: 提出PG-KINN：采用Petrov-Galerkin变分公式，试函数空间为KAN，测试函数空间为独立、紧支撑的分段多项式空间，通过Gauss-Legendre求积进行评估。通过分部积分降低微分阶数，保持对一般非自伴、非线性和反问题的适用性；局部化测试函数将全局残差转化为一组条件良好的单元级弱残差。

Result: 在裂纹奇异性、应力集中、Neo-Hookean超弹性、非均匀介质逆参数识别和复杂几何等基准测试中，PG-KINN持续优于传统MLP基线和最先进的KAN基强形式/能量形式/逆形式方法（PIKAN）。

Conclusion: Petrov-Galerkin耦合KAN试函数空间与多项式测试函数空间是实现鲁棒且精确的AI计算力学的有效路径。

Abstract: Physics-informed learning of partial differential equations (PDEs) has been dominated by multilayer perceptrons (MLPs), whose spectral bias and dense parameterization limit both accuracy and interpretability. Kolmogorov Arnold Networks (KANs) mitigate these limitations because their learnable spline activations are structurally aligned with the piecewise-polynomial bases of classical discretizations. However, the way a PDE is cast into a loss functional is as decisive as the choice of approximator: strong-form residual minimization requires high-order derivatives and heavily weighted losses, the energy (Bubnov-Galerkin) form is restricted to self-adjoint operators and, as we show, collapses to a trivial solution for parameter-identification problems, and boundary integral forms require a known fundamental solution. We propose PG-KINN, a physics-informed KAN built on a Petrov-Galerkin formulation in which the trial space is a KAN and the test space is an independent, compactly supported, piecewise-polynomial space evaluated with Gauss-Legendre quadrature. Integration by parts lowers the differentiation order while retaining applicability to general non-self-adjoint, nonlinear, and inverse problems; the localized test functions turn the global residual into a set of element-wise weak residuals with favorable conditioning. On a suite of benchmarks spanning crack singularities, stress concentration, Neo-Hookean hyperelasticity, inverse parameter identification in heterogeneous media, and complex geometries, PG-KINN consistently outperforms legacy MLP baselines and state-of-the-art KAN-based strong/energy/inverse formulations (PIKAN). These results position the Petrov-Galerkin coupling of KAN trial spaces and polynomial test spaces as a robust and accurate route for AI-based computational mechanics.

</details>

### [91] [Fine-grained Computation-Communication Overlap via Tile-level Signaling and Scheduling for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19539)
*Minyu Cui,Anna Wingkvist,Morgan Ericsson*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种细粒度的生产者-消费者协同设计方案，通过tile级信号和调度将MoE模型中的专家计算与第二次all-to-all通信重叠，在不侵入修改计算算子或通信原语的前提下，显著提升分布式MoE执行效率，在4-A100平台上实现最高2.64x端到端加速和2.74x MoE层加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的MoE实现在专家计算完成后才启动返回all-to-all通信，导致通信延迟处于关键路径上，降低了GPU利用率。

Method: 提出细粒度的生产者-消费者协同设计：(1) 持久化计算内核（生产者）覆盖所有本地专家，消除重复内核启动开销，优先处理远程关键tile；(2) 持久化通信内核（消费者）在小型专用SM分区上运行，当tile就绪时发出segment粒度传输。

Result: 在4-A100 GPU上对三种MoE模型进行评估，相比四种最先进MoE系统，实现最高2.64x端到端加速和2.74x MoE层加速；在不同GEMM形状、路由器模式和生产者/消费者SM分区下均一致提升性能，且保持正确性。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过tile级信号和调度实现了专家计算与通信的有效重叠，避免了侵入式修改，是一种实用且高效的分布式MoE执行优化方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures increase model capacity without proportionally increasing computation cost and have become a key building block for scaling large language models (LLMs) to trillion-parameter regimes. Efficient deployment of these MoE models relies on distributed execution across multiple GPUs, where each MoE layer involves two all-to-all communications: dispatching tokens to expert ranks and returning the expert outputs to their source ranks. Conventional MoE implementations launch this return all-to-all after expert compute completes, exposing communication latency on the critical path and reducing GPU utilization. We present a fine-grained approach that overlaps expert compute with the second all-to-all via tile-level signaling and scheduling. Our producer-consumer co-design combines: (1) a persistent per-rank computation kernel (producer) that covers all local experts on the rank to eliminate repeated kernel launch overhead and prioritizes remote-critical tiles, and (2) a persistent communication kernel (consumer) on a small dedicated partition of streaming multiprocessors (SMs) that issues segment-granular transfers as tiles become ready. Our co-design avoids intrusive changes to the underlying computation operators or communication primitives, making it practical for improving distributed MoE execution efficiency on multi-GPU systems. On a 4-A100 GPU platform, evaluated on three MoE models against four state-of-the-art MoE systems, our approach achieves up to 2.64x end-to-end speedup and 2.74x MoE-layer speedup. Compared with a conventional non-overlap baseline, our approach consistently improves both operator- and MoE-layer-level performance across varying GEMM shapes, router modes, and a broad range of producer/consumer SM partitions, while preserving correctness.

</details>


### [92] [Odin: Primitive-Level Synchronization for Distributed Point-Based Neural Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19893)
*Zhenxiang Ma,Zeyu He,Yuanzhen Zhou,Zhenyu Yang,Yuchang Zhang,Miao Tao,Rong Fu,Jidong Zhai,Hengjie Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Odin是一个分布式点基神经渲染训练系统，通过使用原语级同步替代全局屏障，结合提前调度器与运行时验证机制，在8 GPU上平均提升1.22倍吞吐量、隐藏82%关键路径等待，并在64 GPU场景下相比Grendel最高提升1.89倍，同时保持重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模点基神经渲染（PBNR）在分布式训练中，由于全局任务级或迭代级屏障的存在，同步操作而非渲染本身成为关键路径上的主要瓶颈，限制了训练吞吐量。

Method: 提出Odin系统：1) 用原语级同步替代全局屏障；2) 提前调度器利用稳定局部性和相位顺序识别低冲突重叠窗口；3) 运行时验证原语发布时机，确保后续工作观察到的可变状态正确。提供质量优先路径（保持同步训练可见性）和吞吐优先路径（允许低影响延迟读取）。

Result: 在4种PBNR流水线和13个非城市场景、8 GPU上，平均提升1.22倍吞吐量，隐藏82%关键路径等待。在MatrixCity混合并行64 GPU案例中，相比Grendel提升高达1.89倍，且不改变渲染内核、优化器、训练预算或模型容量。

Conclusion: Odin通过原语级同步策略有效解决了PBNR分布式训练中的同步瓶颈问题，在保持重建质量的前提下显著提升训练吞吐量，具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Point-based neural rendering (PBNR) represents 3D scenes as explicit, trainable primitives and underpins high-quality reconstruction and emerging embodied AI and world-model pipelines. Unlike layer-structured neural networks, PBNR has primitive-indexed dependencies: each view reads and updates only a sparse, view-dependent subset of mutable scene state. As large scenes require distributed training and optimized renderers reduce per-view computation, global task- or iteration-level barriers increasingly place synchronization, rather than rendering, on the critical path. We present Odin, a distributed PBNR training system that replaces global barriers with primitive-level synchronization. Its ahead-of-time scheduler uses stable locality and phase order to identify low-conflict overlap windows, while the runtime validates primitive publication before later work observes mutable state. Odin provides a quality-first path that preserves synchronized-training visibility and a throughput-first path that uses overlap and gradient evidence to admit only small, low-impact delayed reads; structural changes and high-impact cases remain synchronized. Across four existing PBNR pipelines and 13 non-city scenes on 8 GPUs, Odin improves throughput by 1.22 times on average and hides 82% of critical-path wait while preserving reconstruction quality. In a MatrixCity mixed-parallel case study scaling to 64 GPUs, Odin improves throughput over Grendel by up to 1.89 times without changing renderer kernels, optimizers, training budgets, or model capacity.

</details>


### [93] [Ascend to Science: Exploration of AI Chips for Scientific Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20120)
*Weicheng Xue,Kai Yang,Yongxiang Liu,Baisong Xu,Dengdong Fan,Xianglin Liu,Pengxiang Xu,Yonghong Tian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文以Ascend 910 NPU系列为代表性张量计算平台，系统分析了AI加速器在支撑科学计算工作负载时面临的精度、执行和存储层次瓶颈，并通过五项应用研究（HPL-MxP、LRSVD、SGEMM-cube、PQSim、SMC-X）验证了通过异构执行、混合精度数值方法、精度模拟、层次化内存编排和通信-计算重叠等协同优化策略，AI原生NPU在数值鲁棒性、竞争性能和可扩展性方面能够满足科学计算需求，提供了张量中心架构适配科学工作负载的系统性实践案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI加速器（尤其是低精度张量引擎）的飞速发展重塑了计算系统格局，但HPC社区面临一个关键问题：这类硬件在何种条件下能够支持对数值鲁棒性、不规则内存访问和可扩展性有高要求的科学计算工作负载。当前缺乏对此问题的系统性评估和优化指导。

Method: 以Ascend 910 NPU系列为张量中心代表平台，先刻画精度、执行和存储层次三大瓶颈；然后针对五项科学应用（HPL-MxP、LRSVD、SGEMM-cube、PQSim、SMC-X）开发并评估工作负载特定的映射策略，综合运用异构执行、混合精度数值公式、精度模拟、层次化内存编排、通信-计算重叠等技术。

Result: 五项应用研究表明：在数值公式、执行放置和数据搬移得到协调处理的前提下，AI原生NPU能够实现数值鲁棒性、竞争性性能和满意的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该工作为科学工作负载适配张量中心架构提供了最新的实践案例，同时区分了可迁移的通用优化原则与Ascend特定的实现细节。

Abstract: The rapid rise of AI-oriented accelerators has reshaped compute systems around low-precision tensor engines, raising a practical question for the HPC community: under what conditions can such hardware support scientific workloads that demand numerical robustness, irregular memory access, and scalability? Using the Ascend 910 NPU series as a representative tensor-centric platform, we characterize precision, execution, and memory-hierarchy bottlenecks that hinder the direct deployment of scientific codes. We then develop and evaluate workload-specific mappings across five application studies -- HPL-MxP, LRSVD, SGEMM-cube, PQSim, and SMC-X -- combining heterogeneous execution, mixed-precision numerical formulations, precision emulation, hierarchical memory orchestration, and communication--computation overlap. These studies show that AI-native NPUs can achieve numerical robustness, competitive performance, and satisfactory scalability when numerical formulation, execution placement, and data movement are addressed in a coordinated manner. Our results provide a state-of-the-practice case study of how scientific workloads can be adapted to tensor-centric architectures, while distinguishing transferable optimization principles from Ascend-specific implementation details.

</details>


### [94] [Fully Dynamic Rooted Spanning Tree on GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20211)
*Abhijeet Sahu,Harmit Singh,Soham Nandy,G. Ramakrishna*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了四种全新的全动态并行算法，用于在批量边插入或删除时高效更新有根生成森林，无需从头重构。在GPU环境下的实验表明，算法每秒可处理200万次插入和140万次删除，显著优于现有最优的并行静态算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实网络具有动态性，需要高效更新生成树结构。现有并行算法在该问题上几乎空白，而静态重构开销巨大，亟需高效的动态并行解决方案。

Method: 提出了四种全动态并行算法，用于在批量边插入/删除时更新有根生成森林，避免完全重构。

Result: 在GPU环境下的真实图数据集上，达到每秒200万次插入和140万次删除的吞吐量，显著超越现有最优的并行静态算法。

Conclusion: 本文填补了生成森林动态并行更新算法的空白，提出的方法在性能上大幅优于静态重算方案，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Spanning trees are fundamental structures in graph theory, essential for various applications such as network maintenance, routing adjustments, and many more. The dynamic nature of real-world networks requires efficient updates to these structures as the underlying graph evolves. Maintaining rooted spanning trees dynamically is particularly crucial for algorithms addressing 2-connected components and minimum-weighted spanning trees. In this paper, we address the challenge of maintaining a rooted spanning forest when a batch of edges are inserted or deleted. We present four novel fully dynamic parallel algorithms to update the spanning forest without reconstructing it from scratch. To the best of our knowledge, parallel algorithms for this problem remain largely unexplored. Our experiments on a diverse collection of real-world graphs using a GPU environment demonstrate a throughput of 2 million insertions and 1.4 million deletions per second, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art parallel static algorithms.

</details>

### [95] [FineServe: A Fine-Grained Dataset and Characterization of Global LLM Serving Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19349)
*Tiancheng Zhang,Shaoyuan Huang,Mingyuan Wang,Yunfeng Zhao,Xiaofei Wang,Wenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FineServe是一个从全球商业平台收集的多模型LLM服务真实工作负载数据集，揭示了不同模型架构、规模和任务意图下的流量动态和令牌行为差异，并提供了细粒度的工作负载生成器，用于评估路由、调度和容量规划策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务负载研究多依赖代理轨迹或粗粒度表征，无法捕捉现代多模型LLM平台的异构性，缺乏对真实世界中多模型服务动态的深入理解。

Method: 从全球商业平台收集实际多模型LLM服务工作负载数据，进行到达动态和令牌行为的细粒度分析，并开发可配置的细粒度模型感知工作负载生成器。

Result: 揭示了不同模型架构、规模和任务意图下存在根本不同的波动模式；提供了FineServe数据集和工作负载生成器。

Conclusion: FineServe通过暴露细粒度工作负载动态，为评估LLM服务系统中的路由、调度和容量规划策略提供了真实基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as always-on online services, making efficient LLM serving a critical systems challenge. Achieving low latency and high throughput under volatile demand requires deep understanding of real-world serving workloads, yet existing studies often rely on proxy traces or coarse-grained characterizations that fail to capture the heterogeneity of modern multi-model LLM platforms. We present FineServe, an in-the-wild, multi-model LLM serving workload dataset collected from a global commercial marketplace, enabling fine-grained characterization of real-world serving dynamics across heterogeneous models and tasks. Leveraging FineServe, we conduct a comprehensive analysis of arrival dynamics and token behavior, revealing fundamentally different fluctuation regimes across model architectures, scales and task intents. Building on these insights, we develop the FineServe workload generator, which composes fine-grained model-aware workloads into configurable mixtures tailored for benchmarking multi-model serving platforms. By exposing these fine-grained workload dynamics, FineServe provides a realistic foundation for evaluating routing, scheduling, and capacity-planning strategies in LLM serving systems. FineServe is available at https://github.com/hihiztc1/FineServe.

</details>


### [96] [Hybrid LSTM-Graph Neural Framework for Robust Financial Fraud Detection and Adversarial Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19350)
*Mariam Zakaria Moussa Ali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FraudShield AI混合框架，融合LSTM网络与手工构建的图拓扑特征（包括PageRank中心性、入度动态、自定义流量比），结合Focal Loss损失函数和动态阈值机制，在PaySim数据集上显著优于Logistic Regression和XGBoost基线，有效检测洗钱模式中的低值小额交易欺诈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融机构在检测复杂洗钱模式（如smurfing和layering）时面临两个核心挑战：极端数据不平衡（欺诈率仅0.13%）以及不断演化的对抗性规避策略，传统方法难以有效应对。

Method: 提出FraudShield AI混合框架：(1) 利用LSTM网络捕捉交易的时间序列特征；(2) 手工构建图拓扑特征（PageRank中心性、入度动态、自定义Flow Ratio）捕捉结构关系上下文；(3) 采用Focal Loss处理类别不平衡；(4) 引入动态阈值机制增强对低值smurfing攻击的鲁棒性。

Result: 在PaySim数据集上，混合模型在精确率、召回率和F1分数上显著优于Logistic Regression和XGBoost基线，尤其在难以检测的微交易欺诈模式上表现突出。消融研究证实了时间组件和拓扑组件具有互补贡献。

Conclusion: 通过将检测范式从孤立事务分析转向网络级取证，融合时序与图结构信息的混合方法能有效应对洗钱检测中的不平衡数据和对抗性规避问题。

Abstract: Financial institutions face significant challenges in detecting sophisticated money laundering patterns, such as smurfing and layering, due to extreme data imbalance (0.13% fraud rate) and evolving adversarial evasion tactics. This paper proposes FraudShield AI, a hybrid framework that integrates Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks with hand-crafted Graph Topological Features to capture both temporal sequences and structural relational context. By engineering network-centric features including PageRank Centrality, In-Degree dynamics, and a custom Flow Ratio, the system shifts the detection paradigm from isolated transaction analysis to network-level forensics. A Focal Loss objective is used to address class imbalance, and a dynamic thresholding mechanism is introduced to improve resilience against low-value smurfing attacks. Experimental evaluation on the PaySim dataset shows that the proposed hybrid model substantially outperforms Logistic Regression and XGBoost baselines in Precision, Recall, and F1-Score, particularly on hard-to-detect micro-transaction fraud patterns. An ablation study confirms the complementary contribution of both the temporal and topological components.

</details>


### [97] [OpenEvoShield: Dual Non-Stationary Continual Defense for Open-World Multi-Agent System Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19351)
*Litian Zhang,Chaozhuo Li,Yuting Zhang,Zejian Chen,Bingyu Yan,Qiwei Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenEvoShield，一种面向LLM多智能体系统的共进化持续防御框架，通过非对称速率控制、动态行为边界、EWC正则化策略集成和能量基多粒度检测器，在100轮部署实验中显著优于静态和持续基线方法，能检测绝大多数未见攻击并保持低误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统在安全关键应用中面临对手通过智能体间通信注入恶意指令的双重动态威胁：对手不断优化攻击策略，同时正常智能体行为随系统扩展发生漂移。现有防御将部署视为封闭世界问题，一旦分布偏移超出训练覆盖范围，性能就会急剧下降。

Method: OpenEvoShield包含四个模块：M1非对称速率控制器，从双重漂移信号中解耦攻击侧（快）和正常侧（慢）学习速率；M2正常边界更新器，以慢速率维护动态行为边界；M3 EWC正则化策略集成，实现快速适应而不发生灾难性遗忘；M4能量基多粒度检测器，融合节点级、子图级和图级证据将新型攻击分类为分布外样本。

Result: 在5个基准测试和4种MAS拓扑结构上进行了100轮部署实验，OpenEvoShield优于静态和持续基线方法，能检测绝大多数先前未见攻击，同时保持低误报率。

Conclusion: OpenEvoShield通过共进化持续防御框架有效应对LLM多智能体系统中攻击策略和正常行为双重动态漂移的挑战，为安全关键部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (LLM-MAS) are increasingly deployed in safety-critical applications, where adversaries inject malicious instructions through inter-agent communication to propagate harmful behaviors. Unlike static threats, these attacks are doubly dynamic: adversaries refine injection strategies against deployed defenses while normal-agent behavior drifts with system expansion. Existing defenses treat deployment as a closed-world problem and degrade rapidly once either distribution shifts beyond training coverage. We propose OpenEvoShield, a co-evolutionary continual defense framework for LLM-MAS. An asymmetric rate controller (M1) decouples fast attack-side and slow normal-side learning rates from dual drift signals. A normal-boundary updater (M2) maintains a dynamic behavioral boundary at the slow rate, while an EWC-regularized policy ensemble (M3) fast-adapts without catastrophic forgetting. An energy-based multi-granularity detector (M4) fuses node-, subgraph-, and graph-level evidence to classify novel attacks as out-of-distribution. Experiments over 100 deployment rounds across five benchmarks and four MAS topologies show that OpenEvoShield outperforms static and continual baselines, detecting most previously unseen attacks while keeping false positive rates low.

</details>


### [98] [Benchmarking Confidential GPU Inference on NVIDIA H100 under Intel TDX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19353)
*Wei Wang,Abdul Hyee Waqas,Burns Smith*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在Intel TDX机密实例中的单张NVIDIA H100 80GB GPU上，对Mistral-7B和Qwen3-30B-A3B两个LLM进行了机密与非机密推理的基准对比。机密模式下，TTFT增加21.8%-27.8%，全局token吞吐量下降17.7%-21.1%，闭环吞吐量差距在11.5%-20.2%之间，且大模型在机密模式下更早达到饱和拐点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密计算正成为处理敏感输入或保护专有模型资产的AI推理部署的实际需求，但GPU加速的大语言模型服务在启用机密执行后的性能代价尚不明确，且具有工作负载依赖性，因此需要对其进行量化基准研究。

Method: 使用Intel TDX机密实例中的单张NVIDIA H100 80GB GPU，对比标准非机密执行与机密计算模式。选用Mistral-7B v0.1和Qwen3-30B-A3B两个代表性模型，测量首token时间、端到端请求延迟、每请求token生成吞吐量、全局token吞吐量和闭环请求吞吐量，并在递增并发度下进行固定请求率实验和闭环并发实验。

Result: 固定请求率实验中，机密模式使Mistral-7B的平均TTFT增加21.8%，Qwen3-30B-A3B增加27.8%；全局token吞吐量分别下降17.7%和21.1%。闭环并发实验中，吞吐量差距保持在11.5%-20.2%范围，但大模型在机密模式下更早达到饱和拐点。

Conclusion: 机密GPU推理在负载下仍能保持可用的吞吐量，但容量规划必须同时考虑稳态吞吐量惩罚和大模型在机密模式下更早出现的饱和行为。

Abstract: Confidential computing is becoming a practical deployment requirement for AI inference workloads that process sensitive inputs or protect proprietary model assets. However, the performance cost of enabling confidential execution for GPU-accelerated large language model serving remains workload dependent and operationally important. This paper presents a benchmark study comparing standard non-confidential execution with confidential computing mode on a single NVIDIA H100 80GB GPU hosted in an Intel TDX confidential instance. The evaluation uses two representative language models, Mistral-7B v0.1 and Qwen3-30B-A3B, and measures time to first token, end-to-end request latency, per-request token generation throughput, global token throughput, and closed-loop request throughput under increasing concurrency. In fixed request-rate experiments, confidential mode increases average TTFT by 21.8% for Mistral-7B and 27.8% for Qwen3-30B-A3B, while global token throughput drops by 17.7% and 21.1%, respectively. In closed-loop concurrency experiments, throughput gaps remain in the 11.5-20.2% range, but the larger model reaches its saturation knee earlier under confidential mode. The results suggest that confidential GPU inference can retain usable throughput under load, but capacity planning must account for both the steady throughput penalty and the earlier saturation behavior observed for larger models.

</details>


### [99] [FormulaSPIN: Self-Play Fine-Tuning for Natural Language to Spreadsheet Formula Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19354)
*Cy Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FORMULASPIN自对弈框架，利用公式二进制可执行性作为隐式监督信号，通过两玩家博弈和自适应课程学习，在无需额外数据的情况下突破监督微调的性能上限，在电子表格公式生成任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电子表格公式生成方法依赖静态监督数据，在有限标注上迅速饱和；vanilla SPIN因统一惩罚所有不匹配输出，无法区分语义等价变体与真正错误，产生矛盾梯度。

Method: 提出FORMULASPIN框架：(1) 两玩家博弈——主玩家学习偏好当前版本的ground-truth公式而非旧版本输出；(2) 利用二进制可执行性提供隐式监督，将输出按粒度（语义正确→风格优化）排序，实现自适应课程学习；(3) ExecVote：基于执行结果的语义级投票机制，处理多种有效公式变体。

Result: 在NL2FORMULA基准上达到74.9%精确匹配率和87.1%执行准确率，超越传统SFT和前沿闭源模型，性能与使用额外偏好标注的模型相当。

Conclusion: 自对弈框架能有效突破数据稀缺任务的性能天花板，且该方法可扩展到其他可执行领域的任务。

Abstract: Spreadsheet applications are used by hundreds of millions worldwide, yet writing formulas remains a significant barrier. Existing approaches rely on static supervised data, which quickly saturates on limited annotations. In this paper, we introduce FORMULASPIN, a self-play framework that breaks the ceiling of supervised fine-tuning by enabling iterative self-improvement without any additional data. Vanilla SPIN fails on this task: it uniformly penalizes every non-matching output, so execution-equivalent alternatives are punished as negatives in one example while serving as ground truth in another, producing contradictory gradients. Our framework resolves this by exploiting formula generation's unique advantage: binary executability provides implicit supervision that separates semantic errors from valid stylistic variants. We frame training as a two-player game in which the main player learns to prefer ground-truth formulas over those from its previous version, while execution feedback sorts outputs into distinct granularities-enabling an adaptive curriculum that shifts from semantic correctness to stylistic refinement. To further increase accuracy, we incorporate ExecVote, a semantic-level voting mechanism that naturally handles multiple valid formulations. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that FORMULASPIN achieves state-of-the-art performance, with 74.9% exact match and 87.1% execution accuracy on NL2FORMULA, matching models trained with additional preference annotations while outperforming both traditional SFT and frontier proprietary models. These findings underscore self-play's potential to tackle scarce data tasks and open the door to extending it beyond executable domains.

</details>


### [100] [Information Discernment in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19355)
*Joshua Ashkinaze,Laura Kurek,Alina Faisal,Tongyuan Miao,Mariam Joseph,Ceren Budak,Eric Gilbert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了信息辨别能力（Information Discernment）的概念，并构建了Learn2Discern（L2D）基准框架。通过三个规范性公理和可解释性指标，评估LLM是否能够合理辨别信息来源的可靠性（源辨别）以及信息是否使先验更接近真相（真值辨别）。实验涵盖13个模型近67万次试验，发现模型在源辨别和真值辨别上均接近随机水平，倾向于依赖来源流行度而非可靠性，且无论信息是否改善预测，模型更新的程度几乎相同。较新、较大的模型在真值辨别上有所提升，但源辨别未见改进。研究还提出了简单有效的推理时干预措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地使用外部知识源（如互联网），但现有研究缺乏系统性评估模型是否能够合理权衡不同来源的信息可靠性以及信息对先验知识的校正程度。作者希望形式化这一能力并建立可解释的评估框架。

Method: 作者首先提出信息辨别能力的概念，定义三个规范性公理（包括源辨别和真值辨别），并设计可解释的指标。通过预注册的用户研究（n=299）验证公理的外部有效性。随后在13个模型、近67万次试验中系统评估模型的辨别表现，并分析模型规模、版本等影响因素。

Result: 模型在源辨别和真值辨别上均接近随机水平；模型对来源流行度的依赖是可靠性依赖的两倍；无论信息是否改善预测，模型更新程度几乎相同；模型在自身先验已较准确的数据集上整合外部知识效果最好；更大更新的模型改善了真值辨别但未改善源辨别。

Conclusion: 当前LLM存在系统性的信息辨别缺陷，源辨别问题尤为突出且未被模型规模扩展所解决。简单推理时干预可改善辨别能力。随着LLM替代传统搜索引擎，这一对齐属性至关重要。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used with external knowledge sources like the internet. Do they weigh information appropriately -- updating more for reliable sources (source discernment) and more when claims bring priors closer to the truth (truth discernment)? We formalize this as information discernment and introduce Learn2Discern (L2D), an experimental framework and benchmark grounded in three normative axioms with interpretable metrics. To establish external validity, a pre-registered, quota-matched user study (n=299) confirms that real LLM users endorse all three axioms and report that violations reduce their trust and usage intent. Across 13 models and nearly 670K trials, we find consistent failures across both dimensions: models perform near chance on source and truth discernment, rely on source popularity twice as much as source reliability, and update roughly equally whether a claim improves or worsens their position relative to the ground truth. Models integrate external knowledge most effectively on datasets where their priors are already the most accurate. Newer and larger models improve truth discernment but not source discernment, a blind spot that model complexity does not address. We identify simple inference-time interventions that improve both forms of discernment. We release our dataset and survey as a testbed for a core alignment property that scales in importance as LLMs replace traditional search.

</details>


### [101] [NEXUS: Structured Runtime Safety for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19356)
*Elias Hossain,Md Mehedi Hasan Nipu,Tasfia Nuzhat Ornee,Rajib Rana,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NEXUS是一个结构化计划安全监控器，通过结合确定性规则、参数级检查和校准的logistic回归风险评分，对LLM智能体行为执行允许、阻止、请求确认或请求修订四类干预策略。在多个基准上优于纯规则方法，F1达0.949，延迟仅0.205ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用工具的LLM智能体越来越多地执行高风险操作，使得运行时安全监控变得至关重要。

Method: NEXUS采用形式化干预策略，在四种动作（允许、阻止、请求确认、请求修订）间选择；结合确定性安全规则、参数级检查和校准的logistic回归风险评分实现分级升级。

Result: 在128实例合成基准上F1=0.949，四类干预准确率0.6406（优于纯规则27.3个百分点）；R-Judge上F1=0.861 vs 0.849；AgentHarm上与规则持平；IPI上0%攻击成功率（99%控制允许）；NEXUS-Stress上F1=0.881；中位延迟0.205ms，开销低于0.1%。

Conclusion: NEXUS在保持极低延迟的同时显著提升了LLM智能体运行时安全监控的性能，代码和基准已公开。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents increasingly execute high-impact actions, making runtime safety monitoring essential. We present NEXUS (Neural EXecution Utility and Safety), a structured-plan safety monitor that applies a formal intervention policy to select among four actions: allow, block, request confirmation, or request revision. NEXUS combines deterministic safety rules, argument-level inspection, and a calibrated logistic-regression risk score for graded escalation. On a 128-instance synthetic benchmark, NEXUS achieves an F1 score of 0.949 and a 4-class intervention accuracy of 0.6406, outperforming rule-only intervention selection by 27.3 percentage points. It also improves over rule-only on R-Judge (F1 = 0.861 vs. 0.849), matches rule-only on AgentHarm due to threat-model limits, and achieves 0% ASR at 99% control allow on IPI. On the rule-blind NEXUS-Stress benchmark, NEXUS reaches an F1 score of 0.881, highlighting the difficulty of fine-grained intervention routing. With 0.205 ms median latency, NEXUS adds under 0.1% overhead to typical agent loops. Code, benchmarks, and the calibrated risk scorer are publicly released.

</details>


### [102] [Stochastic Primal-Dual Decoding for Multiobjective Generative Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19357)
*Dmitrii Moor,Ben Carterette,Senthilkumar Krishnamoorthy,Kyle Kretschman,Denis Beslic,Melissa Yalla,Alice Y Wang,Mounia Lalmas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级的推理时解码方法，通过在线约束优化和随机原始-对偶近似，在不修改或重训练生成式推荐模型的前提下，实现多目标 slate 生成，并在离线与在线实验中取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐系统在多目标 slate 构建上存在不足：后处理方法未考虑序列生成特性，而将辅助目标直接融入训练在大规模系统中不切实际。需要一种轻量级、无需重训练的解码方法。

Method: 提出推理时解码层，将解码过程建模为在线约束优化问题，通过随机原始-对偶近似方案动态调整相关性与辅助目标之间的权衡，基于剩余约束松弛度（slack）进行顺序物品选择。

Result: 离线实验和大规模在线A/B实验均显示多目标权衡的一致改进，辅助目标提升+1.8%，且用户满意度零损失。

Conclusion: 该方法无需修改或重训练模型即可有效支持生成式推荐系统的多目标 slate 生成，理论上有约束违反和遗憾的保证，实际部署中表现优异。

Abstract: Recent advances in recommender systems (RS) have shown substantial performance gains through generative modelling. In practice, recommendation often involves constructing slates -- ordered lists of items -- that must satisfy multiple objectives beyond relevance, such as constraints defined over item attributes or fairness constraints. Existing multiobjective approaches either rely on post-processing techniques designed for non-generative settings, or incorporate auxiliary objectives directly into model training. The former does not explicitly account for the sequential nature of generative RS, while the latter is often impractical in large-scale systems.
  We propose a lightweight, inference-time decoding layer that augments autoregressive generative RS to support multiobjective slate generation without modifying or retraining the underlying model. We formulate decoding as an online constrained optimisation problem, where items are selected sequentially, and trade-offs between relevance and auxiliary objectives are adjusted dynamically based on the remaining constraint slack, i.e., how much of each objective remains to be satisfied. This is implemented via a stochastic primal-dual approximation scheme that balances relevance and auxiliary objectives during generation.
  We provide theoretical guarantees on constraint violation and regret, and evaluate the proposed approach through extensive offline experiments and a large-scale online A/B experiment in a real-world recommender system. Our results show consistent improvements in multiobjective trade-offs, including a +1.8\% gain in the auxiliary objectives achieved at zero cost to user satisfaction.

</details>


### [103] [LISA: Linear-Indexed Sparse Attention for Efficient Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19358)
*Yu Zhao,Zekun Zhang,Fan Jiang,Bo Zeng,Linlong Xu,Shimin Shan,Yu Liu,Longyue Wang,Weihua Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LISA（线性索引稀疏注意力），一种即插即用的注意力模块，将线性注意力（O(n)）与闪电索引器选择的稀疏自注意力（O(nM)，M<<n）并行融合，通过两阶段蒸馏训练（滑动窗口KL蒸馏+索引器每头KL损失），在DeepSeek-Qwen模型上实现16K上下文50%推理加速且推理性能提升5.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链思维推理模型（如DeepSeek-R1）在test-time scaling范式下推理上下文越来越长，但标准自注意力的O(n²)计算复杂度导致推理成本急剧增长，限制了长CoT推理的生产部署。

Method: (1) 并行双分支结构：线性注意力（长程记忆，O(n)）+ 闪电索引器（选择top-M重要token进行稀疏自注意力）；(2) 门控机制融合两分支；(3) 两阶段训练：阶段1用滑动窗口注意力+知识蒸馏逼近教师全自注意力分布，阶段2用索引器替换滑动窗口，通过每头KL散度损失对齐教师注意力模式。

Result: 在DeepSeek-distilled-Qwen模型上，16K token上下文实现50%推理加速，在AIME和MATH-500等推理基准上平均性能提升5.6%。

Conclusion: LISA通过线性注意力与动态稀疏注意力的有效融合，在不需从头预训练的条件下显著降低长序列推理复杂度，兼顾效率与性能，为长CoT推理模型的生产部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in long chain-of-thought reasoning models such as DeepSeek-R1 have led to increasingly longer inference context lengths under the test-time scaling paradigm. However, the O(n^2) computational complexity of standard self-attention causes inference costs to grow sharply with long sequences, limiting the deployment of long-CoT reasoning in production settings. To address this, we propose LISA (Linear-Indexed Sparse Attention), a plug-and-play attention replacement module that requires no pretraining from scratch. LISA integrates two lightweight components in parallel within the original model: (1) a Linear Attention module that provides long-range memory with O(n) time complexity; (2) a Lightning Indexer that selects the top-M important tokens from the full context to feed into a Sparse Self-Attention. The two branches are fused via a gating mechanism, reducing inference complexity from O(n^2) to O(nM) (M << n) for generating n tokens. We design a two-stage training pipeline: Stage 1 initializes the model by integrating the linear attention to capture long-range dependencies, complemented by a sliding-window attention mechanism that is optimized via knowledge distillation to approximate the full self-attention distribution of a frozen teacher model. In Stage 2, we further introduce the Indexer to replace the static sliding-window mechanism, enabling dynamic token selection from broader contexts. The Indexer is trained using a novel per-head KL divergence loss, which aligns its selection behavior with the attention patterns of the teacher model. Experiments on DeepSeek-distilled-Qwen models demonstrate that LISA achieves a 50% inference speedup under 16K-token context, while improving average performance by 5.6% on reasoning benchmarks including AIME and MATH-500.

</details>


### [104] [Profile-Graph Memory for LLM Agents: Implicit Cross-Entity Traversal through Narrative Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19359)
*Shengtong Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多跳记忆基准MemHop（1,000个问题，1-5跳，10个社交网络场景）和ProGraph双层次记忆架构（档案扩展+压缩残差），在MemHop上达80.1%，LoCoMo上达78.4%，超越Mem0、A-Mem、HippoRAG和RAG等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体长时记忆基准仅评估单跳回忆，缺乏对多跳关联（multi-hop association）的衡量，无法满足跨会话交互场景的实际需求。

Method: 提出ProGraph两层记忆架构：(i) 档案扩展层——通过子串匹配遍历LLM生成叙述中自然出现的实体名，作为显式知识图谱的轻量替代；(ii) 压缩残差层——在每次档案更新时零额外API代价共提取精确日期、数量及命名项。同时构建MemHop基准，含1,000个1-5跳问题及每跳证据标注。

Result: ProGraph在MemHop上平均80.1%（与FullContext参考持平），LoCoMo上78.4%（超出FullContext 11.3pp）。消融实验显示：移除档案扩展导致MemHop下降22.6pp，移除残差共提取导致LoCoMo下降8.6pp，跨效应均低于3pp，表明机制有跨基准的专门化分工。

Conclusion: ProGraph通过双层次记忆架构有效提升了多跳推理和精确召回能力，MemHop为多跳记忆评估提供了标准化基准，两者共同推动了LLM智能体长时记忆的研究进展。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LLM agents that interact across sessions, yet current memory benchmarks primarily evaluate single-hop recall, leaving multi-hop association largely unmeasured. We make three contributions. First, we introduce MemHop, a multi-hop memory benchmark of 1,000 questions at hop depths 1-5 across 10 social-network scenarios, with per-hop evidence annotations. Second, we present Profile-Graph Memory (ProGraph), a two-layer memory architecture combining (i) profile expansion -- substring-matched traversal of entity names that naturally appear in LLM-written profile narratives, a minimal alternative to explicit knowledge-graph construction -- and (ii) compression residuals -- exact dates, quantities, and named items co-extracted with each profile update at zero extra API cost. Third, a full-grid ablation shows cross-benchmark mechanism specialization: profile expansion drives multi-hop reasoning (-22.6pp on MemHop when removed) while compression residuals drive precision recall (-8.6pp on LoCoMo when not co-extracted), with cross-effects under 3pp within a single architecture. ProGraph averages 80.1% on MemHop (matching the FullContext reference) and 78.4% on LoCoMo (exceeding FullContext by 11.3pp), outperforming Mem0, A-Mem, HippoRAG, and RAG on both. We release MemHop, ProGraph, and baseline implementations.

</details>


### [105] [Lifted Representation Hypothesis in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19360)
*Bumjin Park,Jaesik Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“潜表征提升假说”，认为LLM通过共享的潜在结构而非孤立事实更新记忆，并通过实验发现LLM在嵌套规则与例外数据中易出现“过早提升”和“碎裂失败”问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM常通过将个别观测映射到更通用的规则结构来回答问题，但其存储、选择与修订机制尚不明确，因此需要研究这一过程。

Method: 提出潜表征提升假说，通过控制性的例外学习实验，在上下文学习、LoRA和全微调三种设置下评估LLM的提升与碎裂行为。

Result: LLM在嵌套规则与例外数据中容易过早提升，同时面临碎裂失败，即无法将粗粒度提升结构细化为更具体的子类型。

Conclusion: 需深入研究数据与规则结构之间的关系，以理解LLM中规则结构的存储、选择与修订机制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often answer queries by mapping individual observations to more general rule-like structures. However, it remains unclear how these structures are stored, selected, and revised. To study this process, we propose thelifted representation hypothesis: LLMs update memory through shared latent structures rather than isolated instance-level facts. This view frames lifting as an efficient use of symmetry across instances, and shattering as the refinement of coarse lifted structures into more specific subtypes. We evaluate LLMs' lifting and shattering through controlled exception-learning experiments across in-context learning, LoRA, and full fine-tuning. We find that LLMs are vulnerable to shattering failures when data are governed by nested rules and exceptions, while lifting often occurs prematurely. These results highlight the need to study the relation between data and rule structures in LLMs.

</details>


### [106] [GraphContainer: A Unified Platform for Comparing and Debugging Graph RAG Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19362)
*Seonho An,Chaejeong Hyun,Min-Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraphContainer是一个统一和可视化不同图RAG工作流的平台，通过统一图表示层（UGR）标准化多格式图，以及Graph Recorder追踪和可视化检索过程，支持交互式调试和可控比较，降低研究者设计优化图RAG管道的门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图RAG方法高度碎片化且不兼容，不同框架的图格式存在结构异构性，缺乏细粒度的可视化工具，导致评估和比较检索行为极其困难。

Method: 提出GraphContainer平台，包含两个核心组件：(1) 统一图表示层（UGR），可将多格式图标准化；(2) Graph Recorder，追踪并可视化逐步检索过程。通过交互式Web界面实现实时可追溯的可视化调试。

Result: 能够导入异构图并执行实时的、可追溯的可视化调试；支持对不同图格式和检索策略进行可控比较。

Conclusion: GraphContainer降低了研究人员和实践者设计最优图RAG管道的门槛，为图RAG方法的评估和比较提供了统一平台。

Abstract: Graph RAG mitigates hallucinations and stale knowledge in LLMs, particularly for multi-hop question answering. However, existing approaches remain highly fragmented and incompatible. The structural heterogeneity of graph formats across different frameworks and the lack of granular visualization tools make it exceedingly difficult to evaluate and compare retrieval behaviors. To bridge this gap, we propose GraphContainer, a novel platform designed to unify and visualize diverse graph RAG workflows. GraphContainer features two key components: (1) a Unified Graph Representation (UGR) layer that seamlessly standardizes multi-format graphs, and (2) a Graph Recorder that tracks and visually renders the step-by-step retrieval process. Through an interactive web interface, we demonstrate GraphContainer's ability to import heterogeneous graphs and perform live, traceable visual debugging of graph RAG methods. Ultimately, we show how GraphContainer enables controlled comparisons of various graph formats and retrieval strategies, lowering the barrier for researchers and practitioners to design optimal graph RAG pipelines. A demonstration video is available at https://youtu.be/O02eNJLwkU0.

</details>


### [107] [AdaRoPE: Not All Attention Heads Should Rotate and Scale Equally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19363)
*Shaowen Wang,Yuke Zheng,Tansheng Zhu,Shuang Chen,Shaofan Liu,Suncong Zheng,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaRoPE，为每个注意力头分配可学习的旋转频率和注意力缩放因子，以替代传统RoPE中统一的频率调度和缩放策略，在长上下文场景中显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RoPE对所有注意力头施加统一的频率调度和缩放，忽略了不同功能角色的注意力头需要不同的频率范围和缩放因子，导致嵌入维度利用不充分，尤其在长上下文场景下性能下降。

Method: AdaRoPE为每个注意力头配备可学习的旋转频率和注意力缩放因子，通过端到端训练让模型自主学习每个头的最优位置编码参数。

Result: 预训练LLM中AdaRoPE一致优于现有RoPE变体（包括partial RoPE和NoPE基线）；在上下文扩展中，头部特定的缩放优于YaRN等方法的统一缩放，在保持短上下文性能的同时实现更好的长上下文扩展。

Conclusion: 在单个注意力头级别优化旋转位置嵌入至关重要，AdaRoPE通过头级别的差异化设计有效提升了位置编码的表达能力和长上下文适应性。

Abstract: Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) is widely adopted in Transformers to encode positional information, yet standard implementations enforce a uniform frequency schedule and scaling across all attention heads. Using simplified retrieval tasks and length generalization scenarios, we show -- both empirically and theoretically -- that heads with different functional roles require distinct frequency ranges and attention scaling factors to operate effectively. Ignoring this structure leads to suboptimal utilization of embedding dimensions and degraded performance, particularly under long-context settings. To address these limitations, we propose AdaRoPE, which equips each attention head with learnable rotation frequencies and attention scaling factors. Pretrained LLMs with AdaRoPE consistently outperform existing RoPE variants, including partial RoPE and NoPE baselines. For context extension, we further show that uniform frequency and attention scaling, used in methods such as YaRN, are suboptimal. By applying head-specific scaling, AdaRoPE enables better context extension while better preserving short-context performance in both the extrapolation setting and the long-context continued pretraining setting. These results highlight the importance of optimizing rotary position embedding at the level of individual attention heads.

</details>


### [108] [Statistically Grounded Sparse-Feature Interventions for Activation-Space Control in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19364)
*Oshayer Siddique,J. M Areeb Uzair Alam,Md Jobayer Rahman Rafy,Syed Rifat Raiyan,Hasan Mahmud,Md Kamrul Hasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种透明的SAE特征引导管道，通过六条件可靠性过滤和无加权Borda共识排名选择稀疏特征，构建Cohen's-d加权的引导方向。在三个Gemma模型、四个行为域和356个配置上的实验表明，该方法可产生可测量的领域特定偏移，但可用引导高度依赖于模型、域、层和强度，最强配置下逻辑正确性引导在主分数上达到+1.16的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导为大型语言模型的行为控制提供了轻量级替代方案，但基于SAE的引导方法通常依赖学习式引导目标或单一标准特征选择，缺乏透明性和可解释性。

Method: 提出六条件可靠性过滤对SAE特征进行初步筛选，然后通过F检验、KSG互信息和Cohen's d三种互补统计量的无加权Borda共识对稀疏特征进行排序，最终构建Cohen's-d加权的SAE解码器行组合作为引导方向，该方法在近似SAE特征去相关假设下受Fisher-LDA启发。

Result: 在三个Gemma系列模型、四个行为域和356种层-强度配置上验证，最强配置（Gemma 2 9B逻辑正确性引导）达到+1.16的主分数增量；但发现可用引导高度依赖于模型、域、层和强度，且原始属性移动与质量保持生成之间存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 激活引导评估应同时报告质量条件化的成功率和原始行为偏移；该方法虽能产生可测量的领域特定偏移，但其有效性高度局部化，需要针对具体配置进行精细调整。

Abstract: Activation steering offers a lightweight alternative to fine-tuning for behavioral control of large language models, but SAE-based steering methods often rely on learned steering objectives or single-criterion feature selection. We introduce a transparent SAE-feature steering pipeline that first applies a six-condition reliability filter, then ranks sparse features through an unweighted Borda consensus over three complementary statistics: $F$-test, KSG mutual information, and Cohen's $d$. The resulting steering direction is constructed as a Cohen's-$d$-weighted combination of SAE decoder rows, providing an optimization-free direction motivated by Fisher-LDA under approximate SAE-feature decorrelation. Across three Gemma-family models, four behavioral domains, and 356 layer-strength configurations, the method produces measurable domain-specific shifts while revealing a substantial gap between raw attribute movement and quality-preserving generation. In the strongest configuration, logical-correctness steering reaches a primary-score delta of $+1.16$ in Gemma~2 9B; however, our broader finding is that usable steering is highly localized by model, domain, layer, and strength. These results argue that activation-steering evaluations should report quality-conditioned success alongside raw behavioral shift. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/Oshayer-Siddique/LLM-Steering-Using-SAE.

</details>


### [109] [Logic-Guided Data Extraction with Answer Set Programming and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19365)
*Mario Alviano,Lorenzo Grillo,Nicola Leone,Fabrizio Lo Scudo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种逻辑引导的数据提取框架，将LLM提取与回答集编程(ASP)相结合，通过ASP推理来验证、推断和控制LLM的提取过程，减少LLM调用次数并提升提取质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在从非结构化文本中提取语义数据时，难以处理需要复杂组合推理和全局一致性的任务，现有方法对所有目标谓词独立查询LLM，效率低且易产生虚假输出。

Method: (1) LLM生成候选事实，ASP进行验证、推理、一致性检查和过程控制；(2) 利用ASP推理识别当前阶段逻辑上可接受的谓词，引导LLM的提取查询；(3) LLM调用与ASP推导交错进行，自动推断逻辑蕴含的事实；(4) 引入基于单调性的缓存机制减少求解器调用。

Result: 在ASP导出的基准测试中，该框架减少了LLM调用次数，并通过抑制虚假输出提升了提取质量。

Conclusion: 非单调逻辑编程（ASP）对于受控语义提取具有重要价值，该框架在理论上与基线方法等价（在温和假设下），但需要更少的LLM调用。

Abstract: When Large Language Models (LLMs) are used for semantic data extraction from unstructured text, producing candidate relational facts from natural language, they may remain unreliable for tasks requiring complex combinatorial reasoning and global consistency. This paper proposes a logic-guided data extraction framework combining LLM-based extraction with Answer Set Programming (ASP). The LLM produces candidate facts, whereas ASP performs validation, inference, consistency checking, and control. Unlike existing pipelines that query the LLM independently for all target predicates, the proposed approach uses ASP reasoning to identify which predicates are logically admissible at each stage and to guide extraction queries. By interleaving LLM calls with ASP derivation, the framework infers logically implied facts without further extraction and detects inconsistencies early. We formalize the pipeline and prove that, under mild assumptions, it is equivalent to the baseline approach with respect to the final extracted facts, while requiring fewer LLM calls. We also introduce a caching mechanism for logic-based control queries, exploiting monotonicity of conjunctive queries over incrementally constructed fact sets to reduce solver invocations.
